	extern(C) size_t SDL_iconv(SDL_IConv cd, const(char)** inBuf, size_t* inbytesleft, char** outBuf, size_t* outBytesLeft){ return _SDL_iconv(__traits(parameters)); }
	package _pSDL_iconv_string _SDL_iconv_string;
	extern(C) char* SDL_iconv_string(const(char)* toCode, const(char)* fromCode, const(char)* inBuf, size_t inBytesLeft){ return _SDL_iconv_string(__traits(parameters)); }
}

	alias _pSDL_GetPixelFormatName = extern(C) const(char)* function(SDL_PixelFormat format);

	alias _pSDL_GetPixelFormatDetails = extern(C) const(SDL_PixelFormatDetails)* function(SDL_PixelFormat format);
	alias _pSDL_CreatePalette = extern(C) SDL_Palette* function(int nColours);

	alias _pSDL_DestroyPalette = extern(C) void function(SDL_Palette* palette);

__gshared nothrow @nogc{
	package _pSDL_GetPixelFormatName _SDL_GetPixelFormatName;
	extern(C) const(char)* SDL_GetPixelFormatName(SDL_PixelFormat format){ return _SDL_GetPixelFormatName(__traits(parameters)); }
	package _pSDL_GetMasksForPixelFormat _SDL_GetMasksForPixelFormat;
	extern(C) bool SDL_GetMasksForPixelFormat(SDL_PixelFormat format, int* bpp, uint* rMask, uint* gMask, uint* bMask, uint* aMask){ return _SDL_GetMasksForPixelFormat(__traits(parameters)); }
	package _pSDL_GetPixelFormatForMasks _SDL_GetPixelFormatForMasks;
	extern(C) SDL_PixelFormat SDL_GetPixelFormatForMasks(int bpp, uint rMask, uint gMask, uint bMask, uint aMask){ return _SDL_GetPixelFormatForMasks(__traits(parameters)); }
	package _pSDL_GetPixelFormatDetails _SDL_GetPixelFormatDetails;
	extern(C) const(SDL_PixelFormatDetails)* SDL_GetPixelFormatDetails(SDL_PixelFormat format){ return _SDL_GetPixelFormatDetails(__traits(parameters)); }
	package _pSDL_CreatePalette _SDL_CreatePalette;
	extern(C) SDL_Palette* SDL_CreatePalette(int nColours){ return _SDL_CreatePalette(__traits(parameters)); }
	package _pSDL_SetPaletteColors _SDL_SetPaletteColors;
	extern(C) bool SDL_SetPaletteColors(SDL_Palette* palette, const(SDL_Colour)* colours, int firstColour, int nColours){ return _SDL_SetPaletteColors(__traits(parameters)); }
	package _pSDL_DestroyPalette _SDL_DestroyPalette;

import bindbc.loader: SharedLib, bindSymbol;
static void bindModuleSymbols(SharedLib lib) nothrow @nogc{
	alias here = mixin((string mod=__MODULE__){
	static if(__traits(compiles, typeof(this))) return __traits(identifier, typeof(this));
	else return mod;
}());
	lib.bindSymbol(cast(void**)&_SDL_GetNumLogicalCPUCores, here.SDL_GetNumLogicalCPUCores.mangleof);
	lib.bindSymbol(cast(void**)&_SDL_GetCPUCacheLineSize, here.SDL_GetCPUCacheLineSize.mangleof);
	lib.bindSymbol(cast(void**)&_SDL_HasAltiVec, here.SDL_HasAltiVec.mangleof);
	lib.bindSymbol(cast(void**)&_SDL_HasMMX, here.SDL_HasMMX.mangleof);
	lib.bindSymbol(cast(void**)&_SDL_HasSSE, here.SDL_HasSSE.mangleof);
	lib.bindSymbol(cast(void**)&_SDL_HasSSE2, here.SDL_HasSSE2.mangleof);
	lib.bindSymbol(cast(void**)&_SDL_HasSSE3, here.SDL_HasSSE3.mangleof);
	lib.bindSymbol(cast(void**)&_SDL_HasSSE41, here.SDL_HasSSE41.mangleof);
	lib.bindSymbol(cast(void**)&_SDL_HasSSE42, here.SDL_HasSSE42.mangleof);
	lib.bindSymbol(cast(void**)&_SDL_HasAVX, here.SDL_HasAVX.mangleof);
	lib.bindSymbol(cast(void**)&_SDL_HasAVX2, here.SDL_HasAVX2.mangleof);
	lib.bindSymbol(cast(void**)&_SDL_HasAVX512F, here.SDL_HasAVX512F.mangleof);
	lib.bindSymbol(cast(void**)&_SDL_HasARMSIMD, here.SDL_HasARMSIMD.mangleof);
	lib.bindSymbol(cast(void**)&_SDL_HasNEON, here.SDL_HasNEON.mangleof);
	lib.bindSymbol(cast(void**)&_SDL_HasLSX, here.SDL_HasLSX.mangleof);
	lib.bindSymbol(cast(void**)&_SDL_HasLASX, here.SDL_HasLASX.mangleof);
	lib.bindSymbol(cast(void**)&_SDL_GetSystemRAM, here.SDL_GetSystemRAM.mangleof);

import bindbc.loader: SharedLib, bindSymbol;
static void bindModuleSymbols(SharedLib lib) nothrow @nogc{
	alias here = mixin((string mod=__MODULE__){
	static if(__traits(compiles, typeof(this))) return __traits(identifier, typeof(this));
	else return mod;
}());

import bindbc.loader: SharedLib, bindSymbol;
static void bindModuleSymbols(SharedLib lib) nothrow @nogc{
	alias here = mixin((string mod=__MODULE__){
	static if(__traits(compiles, typeof(this))) return __traits(identifier, typeof(this));
	else return mod;
}());
	lib.bindSymbol(cast(void**)&_SDL_PumpEvents, here.SDL_PumpEvents.mangleof);
	lib.bindSymbol(cast(void**)&_SDL_PeepEvents, here.SDL_PeepEvents.mangleof);
	lib.bindSymbol(cast(void**)&_SDL_HasEvent, here.SDL_HasEvent.mangleof);
	lib.bindSymbol(cast(void**)&_SDL_HasEvents, here.SDL_HasEvents.mangleof);
	lib.bindSymbol(cast(void**)&_SDL_FlushEvent, here.SDL_FlushEvent.mangleof);
	lib.bindSymbol(cast(void**)&_SDL_FlushEvents, here.SDL_FlushEvents.mangleof);
	lib.bindSymbol(cast(void**)&_SDL_PollEvent, here.SDL_PollEvent.mangleof);
	lib.bindSymbol(cast(void**)&_SDL_WaitEvent, here.SDL_WaitEvent.mangleof);
	lib.bindSymbol(cast(void**)&_SDL_WaitEventTimeout, here.SDL_WaitEventTimeout.mangleof);
	lib.bindSymbol(cast(void**)&_SDL_PushEvent, here.SDL_PushEvent.mangleof);
	lib.bindSymbol(cast(void**)&_SDL_SetEventFilter, here.SDL_SetEventFilter.mangleof);
	lib.bindSymbol(cast(void**)&_SDL_GetEventFilter, here.SDL_GetEventFilter.mangleof);
	lib.bindSymbol(cast(void**)&_SDL_AddEventWatch, here.SDL_AddEventWatch.mangleof);
	lib.bindSymbol(cast(void**)&_SDL_RemoveEventWatch, here.SDL_RemoveEventWatch.mangleof);
	lib.bindSymbol(cast(void**)&_SDL_FilterEvents, here.SDL_FilterEvents.mangleof);
	lib.bindSymbol(cast(void**)&_SDL_SetEventEnabled, here.SDL_SetEventEnabled.mangleof);
	lib.bindSymbol(cast(void**)&_SDL_EventEnabled, here.SDL_EventEnabled.mangleof);
	lib.bindSymbol(cast(void**)&_SDL_RegisterEvents, here.SDL_RegisterEvents.mangleof);
	lib.bindSymbol(cast(void**)&_SDL_GetWindowFromEvent, here.SDL_GetWindowFromEvent.mangleof);
}

__gshared nothrow @nogc{
	package _pSDL_GPUSupportsShaderFormats _SDL_GPUSupportsShaderFormats;
	extern(C) bool SDL_GPUSupportsShaderFormats(SDL_GPUShaderFormat_ formatFlags, const(char)* name){ return _SDL_GPUSupportsShaderFormats(__traits(parameters)); }
	package _pSDL_GPUSupportsProperties _SDL_GPUSupportsProperties;
	extern(C) bool SDL_GPUSupportsProperties(SDL_PropertiesID props){ return _SDL_GPUSupportsProperties(__traits(parameters)); }
	package _pSDL_CreateGPUDevice _SDL_CreateGPUDevice;
	extern(C) SDL_GPUDevice* SDL_CreateGPUDevice(SDL_GPUShaderFormat_ formatFlags, bool debugMode, const(char)* name){ return _SDL_CreateGPUDevice(__traits(parameters)); }
	package _pSDL_CreateGPUDeviceWithProperties _SDL_CreateGPUDeviceWithProperties;
	extern(C) SDL_GPUDevice* SDL_CreateGPUDeviceWithProperties(SDL_PropertiesID props){ return _SDL_CreateGPUDeviceWithProperties(__traits(parameters)); }
	package _pSDL_DestroyGPUDevice _SDL_DestroyGPUDevice;
	extern(C) void SDL_DestroyGPUDevice(SDL_GPUDevice* device){ _SDL_DestroyGPUDevice(__traits(parameters)); }
	package _pSDL_GetNumGPUDrivers _SDL_GetNumGPUDrivers;

import bindbc.loader: SharedLib, bindSymbol;
static void bindModuleSymbols(SharedLib lib) nothrow @nogc{
	alias here = mixin((string mod=__MODULE__){
	static if(__traits(compiles, typeof(this))) return __traits(identifier, typeof(this));
	else return mod;
}());
	lib.bindSymbol(cast(void**)&_SDL_GPUSupportsShaderFormats, here.SDL_GPUSupportsShaderFormats.mangleof);
	lib.bindSymbol(cast(void**)&_SDL_GPUSupportsProperties, here.SDL_GPUSupportsProperties.mangleof);
	lib.bindSymbol(cast(void**)&_SDL_CreateGPUDevice, here.SDL_CreateGPUDevice.mangleof);
	lib.bindSymbol(cast(void**)&_SDL_CreateGPUDeviceWithProperties, here.SDL_CreateGPUDeviceWithProperties.mangleof);
	lib.bindSymbol(cast(void**)&_SDL_DestroyGPUDevice, here.SDL_DestroyGPUDevice.mangleof);
	lib.bindSymbol(cast(void**)&_SDL_GetNumGPUDrivers, here.SDL_GetNumGPUDrivers.mangleof);
	lib.bindSymbol(cast(void**)&_SDL_GetGPUDriver, here.SDL_GetGPUDriver.mangleof);
	lib.bindSymbol(cast(void**)&_SDL_GetGPUDeviceDriver, here.SDL_GetGPUDeviceDriver.mangleof);
	lib.bindSymbol(cast(void**)&_SDL_GetGPUShaderFormats, here.SDL_GetGPUShaderFormats.mangleof);
	lib.bindSymbol(cast(void**)&_SDL_CreateGPUComputePipeline, here.SDL_CreateGPUComputePipeline.mangleof);
	lib.bindSymbol(cast(void**)&_SDL_CreateGPUGraphicsPipeline, here.SDL_CreateGPUGraphicsPipeline.mangleof);
	lib.bindSymbol(cast(void**)&_SDL_CreateGPUSampler, here.SDL_CreateGPUSampler.mangleof);

import bindbc.loader: SharedLib, bindSymbol;
static void bindModuleSymbols(SharedLib lib) nothrow @nogc{
	alias here = mixin((string mod=__MODULE__){
	static if(__traits(compiles, typeof(this))) return __traits(identifier, typeof(this));
	else return mod;
}());
	lib.bindSymbol(cast(void**)&_SDL_CreateTray, here.SDL_CreateTray.mangleof);
	lib.bindSymbol(cast(void**)&_SDL_SetTrayIcon, here.SDL_SetTrayIcon.mangleof);
	lib.bindSymbol(cast(void**)&_SDL_SetTrayTooltip, here.SDL_SetTrayTooltip.mangleof);

// expansion at C:\Users\zhouh\AppData\Local\dub\packages\bindbc-sdl\2.2.0\bindbc-sdl\source\sdl\package.d(119,1)

private SharedLib lib;

@nogc nothrow{
	void unloadSDL(){ if(lib != bindbc.loader.invalidHandle) lib.unload(); }

	bool isSDLLoaded(){ return lib != bindbc.loader.invalidHandle; }

	LoadMsg loadSDL(){
		enum libNamesCT = ["SDL3.dll"];
		const(char)[][libNamesCT.length] libNames = libNamesCT;

		LoadMsg ret;
		foreach(name; libNames){
			ret = loadSDL(name.ptr);
			if(ret == LoadMsg.success) break;
		}
		return ret;
	}

	LoadMsg loadSDL(const(char)* libName){
		lib = bindbc.loader.load(libName);
		if(lib == bindbc.loader.invalidHandle){
			return LoadMsg.noLibrary;
		}

		auto errCount = errorCount();

		sdl.assert_.bindModuleSymbols(lib);
		sdl.asyncio.bindModuleSymbols(lib);
		sdl.atomic.bindModuleSymbols(lib);
		sdl.audio.bindModuleSymbols(lib);
		sdl.bits.bindModuleSymbols(lib);
		sdl.blendmode.bindModuleSymbols(lib);
		sdl.camera.bindModuleSymbols(lib);
		sdl.clipboard.bindModuleSymbols(lib);
		sdl.cpuinfo.bindModuleSymbols(lib);
		sdl.dialogue.bindModuleSymbols(lib);
		sdl.endian.bindModuleSymbols(lib);
		sdl.error.bindModuleSymbols(lib);
		sdl.events.bindModuleSymbols(lib);
		sdl.filesystem.bindModuleSymbols(lib);
		sdl.gamepad.bindModuleSymbols(lib);
		sdl.gpu.bindModuleSymbols(lib);
		sdl.guid.bindModuleSymbols(lib);
		sdl.haptic.bindModuleSymbols(lib);
		sdl.hidapi.bindModuleSymbols(lib);
		sdl.hints.bindModuleSymbols(lib);
		sdl.init.bindModuleSymbols(lib);
		sdl.iostream.bindModuleSymbols(lib);
		sdl.joystick.bindModuleSymbols(lib);
		sdl.keyboard.bindModuleSymbols(lib);
		sdl.keycode.bindModuleSymbols(lib);
		sdl.loadso.bindModuleSymbols(lib);
		sdl.locale.bindModuleSymbols(lib);
		sdl.log.bindModuleSymbols(lib);
		sdl.main.bindModuleSymbols(lib);
		sdl.messagebox.bindModuleSymbols(lib);
		sdl.metal.bindModuleSymbols(lib);
		sdl.misc.bindModuleSymbols(lib);
		sdl.mouse.bindModuleSymbols(lib);
		sdl.mutex.bindModuleSymbols(lib);
		sdl.pen.bindModuleSymbols(lib);
		sdl.pixels.bindModuleSymbols(lib);
		sdl.platform.bindModuleSymbols(lib);
		sdl.power.bindModuleSymbols(lib);
		sdl.process.bindModuleSymbols(lib);
		sdl.properties.bindModuleSymbols(lib);
		sdl.rect.bindModuleSymbols(lib);
		sdl.render.bindModuleSymbols(lib);
		sdl.scancode.bindModuleSymbols(lib);
		sdl.sensor.bindModuleSymbols(lib);
		sdl.stdinc.bindModuleSymbols(lib);
		sdl.storage.bindModuleSymbols(lib);
		sdl.surface.bindModuleSymbols(lib);
		sdl.system.bindModuleSymbols(lib);
		sdl.thread.bindModuleSymbols(lib);
		sdl.time.bindModuleSymbols(lib);
		sdl.timer.bindModuleSymbols(lib);
		sdl.touch.bindModuleSymbols(lib);
		sdl.tray.bindModuleSymbols(lib);
		sdl.version_.bindModuleSymbols(lib);
		sdl.video.bindModuleSymbols(lib);
		sdl.vulkan.bindModuleSymbols(lib);

		if(errCount != errorCount()) return LoadMsg.badLibrary;
		return LoadMsg.success;
	}
}

static void bindModuleSymbols(SharedLib lib) nothrow @nogc{
	alias here = mixin((string mod=__MODULE__){
	static if(__traits(compiles, typeof(this))) return __traits(identifier, typeof(this));
	else return mod;
}());

// expansion at C:\Users\zhouh\AppData\Local\dub\packages\bindbc-sdl\2.2.0\bindbc-sdl\source\sdl_mixer.d(232,1)

private SharedLib lib;

@nogc nothrow{
	void unloadSDLMixer(){ if(lib != bindbc.loader.invalidHandle) lib.unload(); }

	bool isSDLMixerLoaded(){ return lib != bindbc.loader.invalidHandle; }

	LoadMsg loadSDLMixer(){
		enum libNamesCT = ["SDL3_mixer.dll"];
		const(char)[][libNamesCT.length] libNames = libNamesCT;

		LoadMsg ret;
		foreach(name; libNames){
			ret = loadSDLMixer(name.ptr);
			if(ret == LoadMsg.success) break;
		}
		return ret;
	}

	LoadMsg loadSDLMixer(const(char)* libName){
		lib = bindbc.loader.load(libName);
		if(lib == bindbc.loader.invalidHandle){
			return LoadMsg.noLibrary;
		}

		auto errCount = errorCount();

		sdl_mixer.bindModuleSymbols(lib);

		if(errCount != errorCount()) return LoadMsg.badLibrary;
		return LoadMsg.success;
	}
}

	alias _pTTF_GetTextSubStringForLine = extern(C) bool function(TTF_Text* text, int line, TTF_SubString* subString);

	alias _pTTF_GetTextSubStringForPoint = extern(C) bool function(TTF_Text* text, int x, int y, TTF_SubString* subString);

	alias _pTTF_GetNextTextSubString = extern(C) bool function(TTF_Text* text, const(TTF_SubString)* subString, TTF_SubString* next);
	alias _pTTF_UpdateText = extern(C) bool function(TTF_Text* text);
	alias _pTTF_DestroyText = extern(C) void function(TTF_Text* text);
	alias _pTTF_CloseFont = extern(C) void function(TTF_Font* font);
	alias _pTTF_Quit = extern(C) void function();
	alias _pTTF_WasInit = extern(C) int function();
}

// expansion at C:\Users\zhouh\AppData\Local\dub\packages\bindbc-sdl\2.2.0\bindbc-sdl\source\sdl_ttf.d(432,1)

private SharedLib lib;

@nogc nothrow{
	void unloadSDLTTF(){ if(lib != bindbc.loader.invalidHandle) lib.unload(); }

	bool isSDLTTFLoaded(){ return lib != bindbc.loader.invalidHandle; }

	LoadMsg loadSDLTTF(){
		enum libNamesCT = ["SDL3_ttf.dll"];
		const(char)[][libNamesCT.length] libNames = libNamesCT;

		LoadMsg ret;
		foreach(name; libNames){
			ret = loadSDLTTF(name.ptr);
			if(ret == LoadMsg.success) break;
		}
		return ret;
	}

	LoadMsg loadSDLTTF(const(char)* libName){
		lib = bindbc.loader.load(libName);
		if(lib == bindbc.loader.invalidHandle){
			return LoadMsg.noLibrary;
		}

		auto errCount = errorCount();

		sdl_ttf.bindModuleSymbols(lib);

		if(errCount != errorCount()) return LoadMsg.badLibrary;
		return LoadMsg.success;
	}
}
